// Two peers, one origin, real git transport (tests/helpers/fixtures.ts): the
// Automerge documents ride the repo alongside the markdown projections, and
// one sync tick (reload.ts syncDataDir) merges concurrent work that used to
// need the conflict dialog. Also: markdown-only edits (older app versions,
// GitHub) are absorbed, published tampering is repaired, deletions win.

import { test, expect, beforeEach, afterEach } from "bun:test";
import { mkdtemp, rm } from "node:fs/promises";
import { tmpdir } from "node:os";
import { join } from "node:path";
import { loadFeatureDoc } from "../../src/server/automerge/store";
import { importDrift } from "../../src/server/automerge/driftSync";
import { projectFeature } from "../../src/server/automerge/project";
import { publishFeature, readFeature, saveFeature } from "../../src/server/features";
import { conflictedFiles } from "../../src/server/git";
import { syncDataDir } from "../../src/server/reload";
import { git, stopServers, twoClones } from "../helpers/fixtures";

let root: string;

beforeEach(async () => {
  root = await mkdtemp(join(tmpdir(), "specdriven-amsync-"));
});

afterEach(async () => {
  stopServers();
  await rm(root, { recursive: true, force: true });
});

const SEED = "# Seed\n\n## Story @v1 [proposed]\n\n- line one\n- line two\n";

/** Both clones with the seed feature already imported into a shared doc. */
async function seededClones() {
  const clones = await twoClones(root, { "specs/seed.feature.md": SEED });
  await syncDataDir(clones.a); // imports the seed into a doc and pushes it
  await syncDataDir(clones.b); // pulls the doc: both peers share one lineage
  return clones;
}

/**
 * Two cron ticks, like the real 60s loop needs after a local save: the first
 * refuses to pull over the dirty file and just commits (and its push may be
 * rejected), the second pulls, auto-merges and pushes.
 */
async function settle(dir: string) {
  await syncDataDir(dir);
  await syncDataDir(dir);
}

test("concurrent edits to the same scenario merge with no conflict dialog", async () => {
  const { a, b } = await seededClones();

  // Both peers edit the same line of the same scenario body.
  await saveFeature(a, "specs/seed.feature.md", SEED.replace("- line one", "- line one, from A"));
  await saveFeature(b, "specs/seed.feature.md", SEED.replace("- line one", "- (B) line one"));

  await syncDataDir(a); // pushes A's markdown and ops
  await settle(b); // pull conflicts on the markdown; the doc auto-merges
  expect(await conflictedFiles(b)).toEqual([]);
  await syncDataDir(a); // A pulls the merge back

  const mdA = await readFeature(a, "specs/seed.feature.md");
  const mdB = await readFeature(b, "specs/seed.feature.md");
  expect(mdA).toBe(mdB!);
  expect(mdA).toContain("from A");
  expect(mdA).toContain("(B) line one");
  const docA = (await loadFeatureDoc(a, "specs/seed.feature.md"))!;
  const docB = (await loadFeatureDoc(b, "specs/seed.feature.md"))!;
  expect(projectFeature(docA.doc)).toBe(mdA!);
  expect(projectFeature(docB.doc)).toBe(mdB!);
});

test("a concurrent first import (two minted lineages) converges", async () => {
  const clones = await twoClones(root, { "specs/seed.feature.md": "# Seed\n" });
  const { a, b } = clones;

  // Neither peer has pulled the other's doc: both mint one for the seed.
  await saveFeature(a, "specs/seed.feature.md", "# Seed\n\n## From A @v1\n\nalpha\n");
  await saveFeature(b, "specs/seed.feature.md", "# Seed\n\n## From B @v1\n\nbeta\n");
  await syncDataDir(a);
  await settle(b); // conflicted markdown and docid; both lineages fold
  expect(await conflictedFiles(b)).toEqual([]);
  await syncDataDir(a);

  const mdA = await readFeature(a, "specs/seed.feature.md");
  expect(mdA).toBe((await readFeature(b, "specs/seed.feature.md"))!);
  expect(mdA).toContain("## From A @v1");
  expect(mdA).toContain("## From B @v1");
  const docA = (await loadFeatureDoc(a, "specs/seed.feature.md"))!;
  const docB = (await loadFeatureDoc(b, "specs/seed.feature.md"))!;
  expect(docA.doc.id).toBe(docB.doc.id);
  expect(docA.docId).toBe(docA.doc.id); // the docid file records the winner
});

test("a markdown-only edit from an old-version peer is absorbed three-way", async () => {
  const { a, b } = await seededClones();

  // A (this app version) edits one line locally; B simulates an old app
  // version: plain markdown commit, no chunk files touched.
  await saveFeature(a, "specs/seed.feature.md", SEED.replace("- line two", "- line two, from A"));
  await Bun.write(join(b, "specs/seed.feature.md"), SEED.replace("- line one", "- line one, from B"));
  await git(b, "add", "specs/seed.feature.md");
  await git(b, "commit", "-m", "old-version edit");
  await git(b, "push");

  await settle(a); // pull conflicts; B's edit has no ops: three-way merge
  expect(await conflictedFiles(a)).toEqual([]);
  const mdA = await readFeature(a, "specs/seed.feature.md");
  expect(mdA).toContain("line two, from A");
  expect(mdA).toContain("line one, from B");
  expect(projectFeature((await loadFeatureDoc(a, "specs/seed.feature.md"))!.doc)).toBe(mdA!);
});

test("external drift is absorbed into the doc on a clean pull", async () => {
  const { a, b } = await seededClones();

  // B pushes a markdown-only change; A has no local edits, so the pull is a
  // clean fast-forward and the drift pass absorbs it into the doc.
  await Bun.write(join(b, "specs/seed.feature.md"), SEED.replace("- line two", "- line two, edited on GitHub"));
  await git(b, "add", "specs/seed.feature.md");
  await git(b, "commit", "-m", "external edit");
  await git(b, "push");

  await syncDataDir(a);
  const doc = (await loadFeatureDoc(a, "specs/seed.feature.md"))!.doc;
  expect(doc.specs[0]!.body).toContain("edited on GitHub");
  expect(projectFeature(doc)).toBe((await readFeature(a, "specs/seed.feature.md"))!);
});

// A hand edit may add a chipless heading; it parses as a draft, and drafts
// are doc-only — the scenario is absorbed into the document while the
// projection rewrites the file without it.

export async function twoClones(
  root: string,
  seedFiles: Record<string, string> = DEFAULT_SEED,
): Promise<TwoClones> {
  const template = await templateFor(seedFiles);
  cpSync(template, root, { recursive: true });
  const a = join(root, "a");
  const b = join(root, "b");
  await Promise.all([repointOrigin(a, template, root), repointOrigin(b, template, root)]);
  const originDir = join(root, "origin.git");
  return { a, b, originDir, originUrl: originDir };
}

export async function publishFeature(
  dataDir: string,
  fileName: string,
  content: string,
  scenario?: ScenarioTarget,
): Promise<PublishResult> {
  const name = safe(fileName);
  let published = markForPublish(content, scenario);
  // Checked before the pull: content we would refuse to write must not leave
  // a merge behind for the user to resolve.
  assertFeatureText(name, published);
  await assertPublishedUnchanged(dataDir, name, published);
  // The pre-pull file is the merge base for the user's buffer if the pull
  // brings a conflict this tick can auto-merge.
  const baseText = await readFeature(dataDir, name);
  const pullResult = await pull(dataDir);
  if (!pullResult.ok) {
    // A pull can fail without leaving a merge behind — no credentials, no
    // network, a missing remote. That is an error to report, not a conflict
    // to resolve: a conflict dialog here would claim the file is missing on
    // the remote when the truth is we could not reach the remote at all.
    if (pullResult.conflictedFiles.length === 0) {
      throw new Error(`Publish failed — git pull: ${pullResult.message}`);
    }
    // The merged documents (or a scenario-level three-way merge) resolve
    // what they can before a human is asked to.
    await autoResolveMergeConflicts(dataDir, pullResult.conflictedFiles);
    if (await mergeInProgress(dataDir)) {
      const theirs = (await theirsVersion(dataDir, name)) ?? "";
      return { status: "conflict", theirs, ours: published, message: pullResult.message };

async function autoResolveOne(
  dataDir: string,
  path: string,
  conflictedSet: Set<string>,
): Promise<string | null> {
  const docidOf = featureFileOfDocidPath(path);
  if (docidOf !== null) {
    // Two peers imported the same feature concurrently and minted different
    // identities. The chunks of both lineages merged in this same pull, and
    // Automerge already picked the winning lineage deterministically — the
    // docid file just records that winner's identity.
    const fileName = docidOf;
    const loaded = await loadFeatureDoc(dataDir, fileName);
    if (loaded === null) return "document did not load";
    await resolveConflict(dataDir, path, `${loaded.doc.id}\n`);
    return null;
  }
  // A markdown page — a roadmap page, a task, a notebook entry
  // (specs/app/engineering-notebook.feature.md "Entries are shared through
  // git"): the same decision tree as a feature's below, minus the scenarios.
  // Its document holds both sides' operations when the other peer runs this
  // version; markdown that arrived without operations folds in three-way.
  if (isPageFolderPath(path) && isPageFile(path)) {
    return autoResolvePage(dataDir, path);
  }
  if (!path.endsWith(".feature.md") || path.startsWith(`${AUTOMERGE_ROOT}/`)) {
    return "not a feature file";
  }
  const loaded = await loadFeatureDoc(dataDir, path);
  if (loaded === null) return "no document behind it";
  const theirsText = (await stageVersion(dataDir, path, 3)) ?? (await theirsVersion(dataDir, path));
  if (theirsText === null) return "deleted on one side";
  const baseText = await stageVersion(dataDir, path, 1);
  const base = baseText === null ? EMPTY : parseFeature(baseText);
  const lineageRace = conflictedSet.has(`${docDirRel(path)}/docid`);
  const opsArrived = await theirsBroughtChunks(dataDir, docDirRel(path));

  let doc = loaded.doc;
  try {
    if (lineageRace) {
      const oursText = await stageVersion(dataDir, path, 2);
      if (oursText !== null && !isProjectionOf(oursText, doc)) {
        const own = threeWayFeature(base, docToFeature(doc), parseFeature(oursText));
        if (!own.ok) return own.conflict;
        doc = reconcile(doc, own.feature);
      }
    }
    if (!opsArrived || lineageRace) {
      const merged = threeWayFeature(base, docToFeature(doc), parseFeature(theirsText));
      if (!merged.ok) return merged.conflict;
      doc = reconcile(doc, merged.feature);
    }
  } catch (e) {
    return e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e);
  }
  await saveFeatureDoc(dataDir, path, doc, loaded.heads);
  await resolveConflict(dataDir, path, projectFeature(doc), [docDirRel(path)]);
  return null;
}

 * A page has no scenarios and one projection form, so there is no lineage
 * race to unpick: the merged document is the resolution when the other peer
 * sent operations, and a three-way merge of the markdown when it did not.

/**
 * One drift pass over the whole data repo. Returns a human-readable summary
 * ("" when nothing drifted). Never runs during an in-progress merge — the
 * caller checks — since conflict markers must not be absorbed as content.
 */
export async function importDrift(dataDir: string): Promise<string> {
  return withSpan("sync.import_drift", { "specdriven.dir": dataDir }, async (span) => {
    const files = (await walk(dataDir)).files;
    let imported = 0;
    let absorbed = 0;
    let repaired = 0;
    for (const fileName of files) {
      const text = await readTextFile(`${dataDir}/${fileName}`);
      // Unreadable or malformed markdown never reaches the document.
      if (text === null || featureTextError(text) !== null) continue;
      const loaded = await loadFeatureDoc(dataDir, fileName);
      if (loaded === null) {
        const created = await createFeatureDoc(dataDir, fileName, importFeatureDoc(parseFeature(text)));
        const projection = projectFeature(created.doc);
        if (projection !== text) await writeTextFile(`${dataDir}/${fileName}`, projection);
        imported++;
        log.debug(`imported ${fileName} into a document`);
        continue;
      }
      // A file in the document's other form — external links written by a
      // peer whose setting differs — is not drift (project.ts).
      if (isProjectionOf(text, loaded.doc)) continue;
      const drift = absorbDrift(loaded.doc, text);
      if (drift.refused === undefined) {
        await saveFeatureDoc(dataDir, fileName, drift.doc, loaded.heads);
        await writeTextFile(`${dataDir}/${fileName}`, projectFeature(drift.doc));
        absorbed++;
        log.debug(`absorbed drifted ${fileName} into its document`);
      } else {
        await writeTextFile(`${dataDir}/${fileName}`, projectFeature(loaded.doc));
        repaired++;
        log.warn(`refused drift on ${fileName}; repaired from its document`, errorAttributes(drift.refused));
      }
    }
    // A document whose markdown is gone was deleted externally: the markdown
    // is the interchange format, so the deletion stands.
    let removed = 0;
    for (const fileName of await listFeatureDocs(dataDir)) {
      if (!(await fileExists(`${dataDir}/${fileName}`))) {
        await removeFeatureDoc(dataDir, fileName);
        removed++;
        log.debug(`dropped document for deleted ${fileName}`);
      }
    }
    span.setAttributes({

The next pull on\neither side brings the other's operations, so the ops-arrived branch\nre-projects the merged document on both: byte-identical, whatever `mergeText`\nchose on each side (`seed`) and whatever each side's own bytes were. Two\nfurther pulls, one per peer, converge them. Caveat (REPORT.md): the merged\ndocument is the CRDT merge of two different `updateText` splices, so the\nconverged text can hold a fragment twice \u2014 H2's duplication, healed by the\ndrift pass when nothing is published. -/\ntheorem H3_second_pull_converges (seed seed' : Nat) (fresh fresh' : Nat \u2192 String) (doc : FeatureDoc)\n    (pl pl' : Pulled) (theirs theirs' : Feature)\n    (ht : pl.theirs = some theirs) (ho : pl.opsArrived = true) (hl : pl.lineageRace = false)\n    (ht' : pl'.theirs = some theirs') (ho' : pl'.opsArrived = true) (hl' : pl'.lineageRace = false) :\n    autoResolveOne seed fresh doc pl = autoResolveOne seed' fresh' doc pl' :=\n  T20_same_bytes seed seed' fresh fresh' doc pl pl' theirs theirs' ht ho hl ht' ho' hl'\n\n/-! ## H4 Same-body

====GIT.LEAN
import SpecDriven.Sync.Schema

/-!
# Git: what the model assumes of the git backend

`src/server/git/backend.ts` is the surface the sync tick uses. The protocol
model does not simulate git. It takes what a pull delivered for one feature
path as data (`Pulled`), in the terms `autoResolveOne` reads it:

- `conflicted`: the path is in `conflictedFiles` after the pull.
- `opsArrived`: `theirsBroughtChunks` — the other side added chunk files for
  this document in the same merge.
- `lineageRace`: this feature's `docid` file conflicted in the same merge.
- `base`, `ours`, `theirs`: `stageVersion` 1, 2 and 3 of the path, parsed
  (`theirsVersion` stands in for stage 3); `none` is "absent on that side".

Assumptions about git itself, stated here rather than proved:

1. **Chunk directories merge as a file-set union.** Chunk files are
   content-addressed and write-once (`store.ts`), so two peers never change
   the same chunk path and a merge adds both sides' files. This is what lets
   the store theorems (T2) speak of "the union of chunk files" and what
   `resolveConflict`'s `alsoAdd` relies on when it stages the doc dir into
   the merge commit.
2. **A path identical on both sides merges clean**; a path changed on one
   side only takes that side; a path changed differently on both sides is
   conflicted and the three stages are available. Standard git.
3. **A deletion on one side with no change on the other deletes.** This is
   why "deleted on one side" is left for the dialog and why a compaction's
   deletions of chunk files propagate.
4. **`resolveConflict` on the last conflicted path completes the merge
   commit and pushes**, so a peer that auto-resolved everything leaves no
   merge in progress for the drift pass to skip.
-/
